## Surveys

All entries in this section: unspecified.

| Date | Resource | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2023‑10‑20 | [Vision-Based Mobile App GUI Testing: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.13518) | Surveys vision-based mobile app GUI testing across test generation, record-and-replay and testing frameworks, drawing on 271 papers including 92 vision-based studies. |
| 2023‑05‑29 | [Task Automation Intelligent Agents: A Review](https://www.researchgate.net/publication/371143847_Task_Automation_Intelligent_Agents_A_Review) | A literature review examines mobile phone task automation agents, including programming by demonstration and usability heuristics for recurring user tasks. |
| 2021 | [30 Years of Automated GUI Testing: A Bibliometric Analysis](https://link.springer.com/chapter/10.1007/978-3-030-85347-1_34) | A bibliometric review traces automated GUI testing research from 1990 to 2020, examining publication growth, citation patterns, collaboration, and emerging mobile testing topics. |
| 2021 | [Robotic Process Automation and Artificial Intelligence in Industry 4.0 – A Literature review](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1877050921001393) | A literature review examines how robotic process automation tools combine with AI for organizational workflows in Industry 4.0, including information extraction and process optimization. |
| 2020‑07‑27 | [From Robotic Process Automation to Intelligent Process Automation: Emerging Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2007.13257) | A survey of robotic and intelligent process automation traces the use of machine learning and AI in business workflows and identifies open research challenges. |
| 2020‑02 | [Robotic Process Automation: Contemporary themes and challenges](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/abs/pii/S0166361519304609) | A structured review of robotic process automation examines software bots that mimic user actions and identifies research themes and challenges for organizational use. |
| 2018‑12‑30 | [A Systematic Literature Review of Automated Techniques for Functional GUI Testing of Mobile Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/1812.11470) | Surveys automated functional GUI testing techniques for mobile apps, comparing the effectiveness, efficiency and practicality of approaches reported in 25 studies. |

## Papers

| Date | Resource | Environments | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026‑11‑01 | [Interface Quilt: Modifying Applications with Computer Use Agents and Generative UI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01891) | unspecified | Introduces Interface Quilt, which uses computer-use agents and generated UI overlays to modify how existing applications look and behave without source access. |
| 2026‑10‑19 | [Step-Aligned Script Generation with Verification and Recovery for Web Automation Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25601) | web | Presents TESSARA, which turns natural-language web test intent into Playwright scripts, linking each browser interaction to selector evidence, outcomes, and repair history. |
| 2026‑10‑13 | [Interaction-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17895) | web, desktop | Uses computer-use agents to interact with generated applications, studying whether interaction improves preference judging, requirement checks, and feedback for application generation. |
| 2026‑10‑02 | [When to Compile a Computer-Use Agent? Measuring Payback and Making Compilation Decisions for Token Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02932) | unspecified | Introduces PACE, a protocol and online decision rule for deciding when repeated GUI procedures are worth compiling into programs. It accounts for failed compilation attempts, measured per-use savings, uncertain reuse, and execution costs under an explicit cumulative budget. |
| 2026‑10‑01 | [GUI-HARVEST: Self-Improving GUI Agents through Evidence-Driven Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00948) | desktop, windows | Improves GUI-agent execution code by analyzing action effects and repeated-task failures, then validating bounded changes with frozen model weights. |
| 2026‑09‑29 | [Neuro-Symbolic Computer Use: Learning Reusable Policies for Reliable and Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36927) | desktop | Learns reusable computer-use policies that encode stable workflow logic in programs while delegating grounding and state checks to neural models. | 2024‑07‑03 | [Seeing is Believing: Vision-driven Non-crash Functional Bug Detection for Mobile Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03037) | unspecified | Trident combines exploration, monitoring, and detection agents to identify functional bugs in a mobile app. It reasons over screenshot sequences and interface transitions to construct test oracles, evaluated on 590 non-crash bugs and additional live apps. |
| 2024‑06‑21 | [SpreadsheetBench: Towards Challenging Real World Spreadsheet Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14991) | unspecified | SpreadsheetBench evaluates LLM spreadsheet manipulation with 912 questions from Excel forums and multiple spreadsheet files as test cases for each instruction. |
| 2024‑06‑12 | [Practical, Automated Scenario-based Mobile App Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08340) | unspecified | ScenTest builds scenario-specific event knowledge graphs from crowdsourced test reports containing GUI and action information. It uses the current app state and testing context to generate scenario tests across applications. |
| 2024‑05‑21 | [SmartFlow: Robotic Process Automation using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12842) | unspecified | Presents SmartFlow, which converts visible GUI elements into text for language-model action planning and executes the plan through a scripting engine. A collection of enterprise applications evaluates adaptation to different layouts and tasks. |
| 2024‑05‑03 | [Automating the Enterprise with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03710) | unspecified | ECLAIR uses multimodal foundation models to automate enterprise workflows from natural-language descriptions, with initial studies of workflow understanding and completion. |
| 2024‑04‑28 | [MMAC-Copilot: Multi-modal Agent Collaboration Operating Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18074) | desktop | Introduces MMAC-Copilot, which coordinates agents with different expertise to operate PC applications. VIBench evaluates visual interaction with applications that lack usable APIs, spanning office, recreation and game tasks. |
| 2024‑04‑19 | [Auto-Formula: Recommend Formulas in Spreadsheets using Contrastive Learning for Table Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12608) | unspecified | Auto-Formula recommends formulas for target spreadsheet cells by adapting formulas from similar spreadsheets within an organization. |
| 2024‑04‑19 | [AutoScraper: A Progressive Understanding Web Agent for Web Scraper Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12753) | web | Introduces AutoScraper, a two-stage framework that uses HTML structure and similarities across pages to generate scrapers for data collection from websites. |
| 2024‑04‑13 | [Enhancing Mobile "How-to" Queries with Automated Search Results Verification and Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08860) | android | Executes instructions from mobile how-to search results inside a controlled Android environment, then uses the observed success of those instructions to verify and rerank technical-support results. |
| 2024‑04‑13 | [Large Language Models for Mobile GUI Text Input Generation: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08948) | android | Studies language-model generation of mobile text inputs, comparing UI representations, execution feedback, and human assistance for app testing. |
| 2024‑03‑17 | [FlowMind: Automatic Workflow Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13050) | unspecified | FlowMind generates API-based workflows from user requests for process automation and presents workflow descriptions that users can inspect and comment on. |
| 2024‑02‑09 | [ScreenAgent: A Vision Language Model-driven Computer Control Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07945) | desktop | Combines a screenshot-and-action dataset with a planning, acting, and reflecting loop for computer control through mouse and keyboard actions. |
| 2024‑02‑08 | [UFO: A UI-Focused Agent for Windows OS Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07939) | windows, desktop | UFO uses two agents to inspect Windows application interfaces and their control metadata, then grounds and executes user requests within and across applications. The study evaluates this UI-focused architecture on nine Windows applications. |
| 2023‑12‑15 | [UINav: A Practical Approach to Train On-Device Automation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.10170) | unspecified | Introduces UINav, a demonstration-based method for training mobile UI automation agents, with a referee model for failure feedback and automatic augmentation of examples. |
| 2023‑12‑04 | [Intelligent Virtual Assistants with LLM-based Process Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.06677) | unspecified | Presents LLMPA, a mobile process-automation architecture that decomposes requests, identifies interface elements, predicts actions and checks errors. Demonstrations evaluate multistep operations in Alipay. |
| 2023‑11‑15 | [Autonomous Large Language Model Agents Enabling Intent-Driven Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.08649) | android | DroidAgent uses language models and memory to set and execute GUI test goals in Android apps; an evaluation covers 15 apps from the Themis benchmark. |
| 2023‑11‑02 | [ProAgent: From Robotic Process Automation to Agentic Process Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.10751) | unspecified | Introduces ProAgent, an LLM-based agent that builds automation workflows from human instructions and coordinates specialized agents during workflow execution. |
| 2023‑10‑24 | [Make LLM a Testing Expert: Bringing Human-like Interaction to Mobile GUI Testing via Functionality-aware Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.15780) | unspecified | Introduces GPTDroid for mobile GUI testing: it feeds app page information to an LLM, runs generated test scripts and uses feedback and memory to guide further exploration. |
| 2023‑10‑23 | [InstructExcel: A Benchmark for Natural Language Instruction in Excel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.14495) | unspecified | Introduces InstructExcel, a benchmark for generating Excel OfficeScripts from natural-language requests, built using scripts recorded from user actions and a collection of existing spreadsheets. |
| 2023‑10‑11 | [CrashTranslator: Automatically Reproducing Mobile Application Crashes Directly from Stack Trace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.07128) | android | Introduces CrashTranslator, which predicts GUI exploration steps from stack traces with an LLM and uses RL-guided search to reproduce crashes in Android apps. |
| 2023‑10‑03 | [AXNav: Replaying Accessibility Tests from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.02424) | unspecified | Introduces an LLM and pixel-based UI system that executes natural-language accessibility tests, records navigable videos and flags potential interface issues. |
| 2023‑09‑11 | [GUI-Based Software Testing: An Automated Approach Using GPT-4 and Selenium WebDriver](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10298721) | web | A web GUI testing method combines GPT-4 with Selenium WebDriver and compares its branch coverage with random monkey testing on a web calculator. |
| 2023‑06‑03 | [Prompting Is All You Need: Automated Android Bug Replay with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01987) | android | Introduces AdbGPT, a prompting-based approach that uses LLMs to replay app bugs from written reports, applying few-shot examples and reasoning without model training. |
| 2023‑06‑02 | [Responsible Task Automation: Empowering Large Language Models as Responsible Task Automators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.01242) | unspecified | Presents ResponsibleTA, a coordinator-executor framework that checks command feasibility and task completion while using local memory for privacy-related controls. The paper compares prompted and learned implementations and evaluates them on UI task automation. |
| 2023‑04‑14 | [DroidBot-GPT: GPT-powered UI Automation for Android](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.07061) | android | Transforms Android UI states and available actions into language prompts for an LLM to choose and execute task steps. |
| 2023‑04 | [Automating GUI-based Software Testing with GPT-3](https://ieeexplore.ieee.org/abstract/document/10132233) | desktop, cross-platform | A GPT-3-based GUI testing method interprets natural-language test cases, tracks current interface elements in prompts, and navigates desktop and mobile applications. |
| 2023‑01‑31 | [FLAME: A small language model for spreadsheet formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.13779) | unspecified | Introduces FLAME, a model trained on Excel formulas for spreadsheet formula repair, completion and retrieval, supporting formula authoring in spreadsheet software. |
| 2022‑12‑09 | [Fill in the Blank: Context-aware Automated Text Input Generation for Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.04732) | unspecified | Introduces QTypist, an LLM-based method that generates text inputs from mobile GUI context and integrates with automated testing tools to help them reach further app pages. |
| 2022‑08‑11 | [CORNET: Learning Table Formatting Rules By Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.06032) | unspecified | Introduces CORNET, a system that learns spreadsheet conditional-formatting rules from cells formatted by users, using symbolic rule enumeration and a neural ranker. |
| 2022‑02‑16 | [A data-driven approach for learning to control computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.08137) | web | Combines reinforcement learning with behavioral priors from human interactions to learn computer control through keyboard and mouse actions. |
| 2018 | [Rousillon: Scraping Distributed Hierarchical Web Data](https://doi.org/10.1145/3242587.3242661) | web | Introduces Rousillon, which generalizes a demonstration of collecting one hierarchical data row into a browser-automation program that traverses related pages. The paper develops relation-selection and generalization methods and evaluates users building such scripts. |
| 2010‑04 | [GUI Testing Using Computer Vision](https://doi.org/10.1145/1753326.1753555) | unspecified | Presents visual GUI test scripts that use images to specify actions and expected screen feedback. Recording both input events and screenshots can generate tests from demonstrations, supporting unit, regression, and test-driven workflows. |
| 2009‑10 | [Sikuli: Using GUI screenshots for search and automation](https://doi.org/10.1145/1622176.1622213) | unspecified | Introduces screenshot-based GUI search and visual scripting, using image patterns to locate interface targets and direct mouse and keyboard events. It evaluates screenshot queries and demonstrates automation and interactive-help tasks. |
| 1991 | [Eager: Programming Repetitive Tasks by Example](https://acypher.com/Publications/CHI91/EagerCHI.html) | desktop | Describes Eager, which detects repeated HyperCard interactions, generalizes them into loops, and previews predicted actions by highlighting interface objects. Users can then ask it to execute the inferred repetitive task. |
